Check out the decklists from the Team Standard Super League! To learn more about how the league works, [click here](https://vintagesuperleague.com/2018/05/05/team-standard-super-league/). You can see these decks in action Tuesdays at 6 p.m. PT on [twitch.tv/magic](https://www.twitch.tv/magic).

* * *

# Week Eight

## Genesis

*Team members: Brad Nelson, Brian Braun-Duin, and Corey Baumeister*
